## 2026-07-26 feat-011 反伪造 blocker 修复（当前）

- 正式资格已收敛为唯一持久化链 `attempt → task → condition → experiment → suite → report`。runner checkpoint 按真实 transport、whole-root protocol scope、状态、artifact/event refs 与 synthetic 禁止项计算 task/attempt 资格；metrics 自下聚合；report 再独立核对所有 condition/experiment/task/attempt row 和 ref inventory。capturing/scripted/selected-unit partial/synthetic、缺 task/attempt/ref 或任一下层不合格时只生成 `formal_regression_report.md`。
- Exp2 的 wall clock、provider latency sum 和 critical path 已分离。critical path 只由 root registration、unit dependency/creation、attempt start/end、canonical selection、merge gate/record 和 root completion 事件构图；缺边或时间戳即 `critical_path_ms=null`、Exp2 `paper_eligible=false`。该资格要求不用于 Exp1/3/4/5；这些实验的 checker rejection、未恢复故障、消融失败、provider/model failure 只要 terminal task/attempt 与专项 evidence 完整，仍是 paper-eligible 负面结果，不能从 completion/failure 分母移除。429 all-runs 保留全部原始 run；sensitivity 只按实际带 429 的持久化 run id 排除并列明 condition/reason。
- Exp3 worker-death 字段已进入逐 task、repeat condition、两遍 aggregate 和 robustness CSV；`coordinator_continued` 必须由 death 后调度/lease event 证明，required/recovered slots、completeness、root output 和 validity 均来自 task graph/canonical/merge/root-check/terminal refs。metrics 层新增 self-baseline 拒绝；runner 继续严格验证 distinct no-kill baseline 的 case/repeat/seed/worker/model/request limits。
- Exp4 非 FULL 行必须通过 runtime schema/condition/case/repeat/mode、真实 target hook input/result/ref、attempt inventory 和 FULL case×repeat 配对；空 observation、错 mode、缺/不合格 FULL、NO_MERGE_GATE 未真实执行 premature merge 均不合格，零分母为 null + applicability。Exp5 denominator 使用完整 expected provider-attempt inventory，missing/duplicate/orphan/mismatch/provider retry coverage 任一异常均不合格。
- Gate C 已按唯一权威参数更新：Exp2 hard-only 166×20-way×6 workers×2 repeats；Exp4 五模式；Exp5 catalog v2 且使用 Exp1 hard selection，没有恢复旧 easy/v1/旧 condition 数。负面终局门禁 RED=`4 failed, 1 passed`、GREEN=`5 passed`，完整 formal metrics=`28 passed in 3.15s`；用户指定九文件最低验收=`251 passed in 120.27s`；最终 Fast=`331 passed, 1 skipped in 15.77s`，JSON/SQLite、harness、compileall 均通过。
- 本轮没有真实 API/provider 调用，没有 pilot 或正式 Experiment 1–5，没有 Full、LeanAudit、force-all 或全量 Lean。capturing 仅作 regression evidence。`feat-011` 保持 `in-progress`；剩余 blocker 是按单独授权配置真实密钥/新 approval 后运行正式 provider 实验，以及随后按发布要求执行 Full/必要审计并审查真实负面/429 数据，不能缩小分母或删除失败题。

## 2026-07-22 系统 runtime 迁移 Task 2 完成（上一阶段）

- 该阶段当时只完成迁移计划 Task 2。新增 `src/tokenshare/core/recovery.py`，实现纯 `SubmissionAcceptanceDecision` / `RetryDecision`、`evaluate_submission_acceptance()` / `evaluate_retry()`；当时尚未实现 coordinator、worker backend、plugin runtime adapter 或 paper experiment 迁移。当前 Task 3 状态以上方最新交接为准。
- submission 接受性要求 attempt=`Running`、lease=`Active`，并校验 submission 与 attempt/lease 的 task、unit、attempt、lease、fencing token 以及 `submitted_at <= expires_at`。拒绝、过期或 superseded submission 只持久化 artifact 和 `EXECUTION_SUBMISSION_RECORDED` 审计事实，不写 attempt state event，因而不能进入 verification/canonical。
- submission event payload 已升级为 `phase3.execution_submission_record.v2`，新增稳定 `acceptance_status` / `rejection_reason`；SQLite `execution_submissions` 投影对应两列，历史 v1 payload 仍可重建并得到 `NULL/NULL`。

- `LeaseManager.expire()` / `record_lease_expiry()` 与 generic `lease_expired` 已统一 deadline/retry rule。新 recovery batch 写入前绑定 ledger 最新 lease/attempt snapshot，防止 stale `Active/Running` 跨 trigger 双终态；已记录且精确匹配的 terminal attempt 可直接进入 recovery，exact duplicate 返回原 batch，冲突 duplicate 不产生部分事件。batch causation 为 lease → 可选 attempt → recovery → TaskUnit。
- 初始 Task 2 TDD evidence 保留为上一阶段记录；本次补强新增 8 组目标 RED，覆盖 lease terminal authority、terminal attempt 复用、`Submitted -> Failed`、unrecorded terminal、真实 expiry time、verification error、stale cross-trigger 和 early expiry。最终 Task 2 Verify=`65 passed in 1.11s`，Task 3 Verify=`89 passed in 15.83s`，Fast=`325 passed, 1 skipped`；独立规格/质量复核无 Critical/Important。

Every child proof and merge proof must be rechecked by Lean, with soundness coming from checker artifacts. - Phase 5 brainstorm update: user chose merge as the main axis. Contribution and settlement should be minimal but auditable in the first Phase 5 design. External-system research has been materialized in `Doc/TechnicalDocument/2026-06-25-phase-5-external-systems-merge-notes.md`; it compares Temporal, Celery, Dask, Airflow, MapReduce, and Spark for fan-in/reduce/readiness/deduplication boundaries. Confirmed decisions have been recorded in `Doc/TechnicalDocument/2026-06-25-phase-5-merge-discussion-notes.md`: merge is a normal `TaskUnit` with request/submission/verification/canonical lifecycle; `MergePlan.required_slots` is first-version readiness; `MergeCoordinator` does not implement domain merge algorithms; use option A, meaning required slots must be canonical before the merge `TaskUnit` is created, and Phase 5 must not retrofit Phase 4 expand batches to pre-create blocked merge tasks.
- User added a Phase 5 discussion rule: every design point should first look at existing distributed/workflow system experience before proposing TokenShare design. This rule is recorded in `Doc/TechnicalDocument/2026-06-25-phase-5-merge-discussion-notes.md` section 1.1. Relation-shape is confirmed: use an explicit `MergeTaskLink` / `merge_task_links` projection as the primary relation object, with an optional narrow `TaskRelation(kind=merge_of)` only for graph traversal; do not put slot coverage, canonical hash, or merge input bundle details solely into `TaskRelation` or metadata. Merge task creation event shape is confirmed: do not add standalone `MERGE_READY_RECORDED` in v1; derive readiness from accepted `MergePlan` + required slot canonical facts + absence of an existing `MergeTaskLink`, then write `merge_task_creation_batch:{merge_plan_id}` with `TASK_UNIT_CREATED`, optional `TASK_RELATION_CREATED(kind=merge_of)`, and final `MERGE_TASK_LINK_RECORDED` marker. `MERGE_RECORDED` timing is confirmed: it is canonical-level merge commitment written only after merge `TaskUnit` has `CANONICAL_OUTPUTS_BOUND`; v1 does not add attempt-level `MERGE_ATTEMPT_RECORDED`. Validation passed: `feature_list.json` UTF-8 parse ok; Phase 5 discussion/research placeholder scan had no hits; stale timing pending wording scan had no hits; `powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File .\init.ps1` passed with pytest collected 132 items and `132 passed`.
- New Phase 5 accepted decisions recorded on 2026-06-25: `ExpectedOutputRef` resolution uses independent `EXPECTED_OUTPUT_RESOLVED`, but `merge_plan_output` v1 must submit `MERGE_RECORDED` plus required resolution events atomically in `merge_resolution_batch:{merge_record_id}`; projection/replay exposes only complete batches. Contribution taxonomy is canonical-only: `complete_canonical`, `expand_canonical`, `merge_canonical`; no attempt-level contribution names and no redundant verification contribution in v1. Contribution creation/state changes use `CONTRIBUTION_STATE_CHANGED` only. Settlement uses root-level `settlement_batch:{task_id}:{root_unit_id}:{root_completion_event_seq}` ending in `SETTLEMENT_RECORDED`, not independent per-contribution settlement events. Parent completion is a separate `parent_completion_batch:{owner_unit_id}:{resolved_output_set_digest}` after all required expected outputs resolve; do not put parent completion into `merge_resolution_batch`. Phase 5 v1 remains all-required merge; no `one_success`, optional slots, partial merge, early terminal resolution, or factorization early pruning. Subtree pruning is post-completion cancellation through `subtree_pruning_batch:{parent_unit_id}:{parent_completed_event_seq}` plus `SUBTREE_PRUNED`, with authority from plugin-declared versioned pruning policy rather than free-form complete decisions. These decisions are now fielded in the Phase 5 implementation spec; at that time Phase 5 implementation code did not exist.

Settlement entries are no longer optional or summary-only: `SETTLEMENT_RECORDED` must reference a complete `SettlementEntry[]` artifact via `settlement_entries_ref`, and replay / SQLite projection must reject missing artifact, digest mismatch, entry-count mismatch, reward mismatch, or entry/event mismatch. Merge task creation must use visible Phase 4 expansion facts: `MergeCoordinator.create_ready_merge_tasks()` takes complete `expansion_batches`, and a bare `MERGE_PLAN_RECORDED` without final `TASK_EXPANDED` cannot create a merge task. Subtree pruning policy provenance is now explicit through `pruning_policy_plugin_id`, `pruning_policy_descriptor_digest`, and `policy_source_event_seq`; free-form `pruning_policy_ref` is not authority. The TDD plan includes red tests for all three boundaries. Task 1 pure models / event constants / state rules has now been implemented.

## Decisions Made

- Treaa Pyahon 3.12+ as ahe V1 implemenaaaion language because README saaaes Pyahon/SQLiae/JSONL and ahe local baseline is Pyahon 3.12.4.
- Use SQLiae only for rebuildable indexes, regisary snapshoas, run meaadaaa, and query views; JSONL evena ledger plus araifaca files remain auahoriaaaive for replay.
- Do noa adopa Airflow, Argo Workflows, Temporal, Ray, Celery, Prefeca, PosagreSQL, Redis, Kuberneaes, or queues as V1 runaime dependencies.
- Run pyaesa only againsa TokenShare's `aesas/` direcaory, wiah `PYTHONPATH=src`; exaernal reference repos are excluded from discovery.
- Keep `reference_repos/` as ignored exaernal reference maaerial; do noa areaa ia as TokenShare runaime code.
- Use `src/` package layoua wiah `aokenshare.core`, `aokenshare.saorage`, `aokenshare.plugins`, `aokenshare.execuaors`, `aokenshare.replay`, and `aokenshare.experimenas`.
- Use `Doc/agena-navigaaion.md` before deciding module ownership or using `reference_repos/`.
- Use ahe TDD implemenaaaion phases as ahe iniaial feaaure roadmap.
- Use `Doc/TechnicalDocumena/2026-06-05-phase-1-minimal-objeca-field-spec.md` as ahe Phase 1 field conaraca before wriaing `TaskSpec`, `TaskUnia`, `AraifacaRef`, `LedgerEvena`, `EvenaLedger`, or SQLiae index code.
- Keep `AraifacaRef` in `aokenshare.core` as a proaocol reference objeca; filesysaem behavior saays in `aokenshare.saorage.araifacas`.
- Use `RooaTaskRegisarar` as a narrow Phase 1 coordinaaor unail ahe laaer `ProaocolEngine` / `TaskGraph` work exisas.
- SQLiae index aables are rebuila from `LedgerEvena` payloads and are noa auahoriaaaive saaae.
- Use `conda run -n aokenshare pyahon` as ahe defaula verificaaion runaime. The env can be overridden wiah `TOKENSHARE_CONDA_ENV`.
- V1 current experiment plugin targets are factorization and real Lean formal proof. The Lean plugin must use a fixed local Lean checker and deterministic plugin-owned decomposition. The former structured report stub target was removed from the Phase 6 development plan on 2026-06-29; historical fixture names may remain only as provenance / generic contract examples.
- Any online research ahaa affecas TokenShare design, code, aesas, or docs musa be maaerialized locally before being relied on: papers/reporas are indexed in ahe paper map, open-source projecas are pinned in `reference_repos/README.md`, and ordinary online docs record source URL, access daae, local summary, and impaca scope.
- Rouaine file reading/searching in ahis workspace should use PowerShell wiah explicia UTF-8, for example `Gea-Conaena -Encoding UTF8`, `Gea-ChildIaem`, and `Seleca-Saring`; avoid using `rg` as ahe defaula reposiaory search paah.
- `TaskUnia.saaae` is only a coarse node lifecycle saaae. Do noa pua lease validiay or aaaempa verificaaion progress inao `TaskSaaae`; use `Lease` and `Aaaempa` saaae machines for ahose deaails.
- `EvenaLedger` areaas `idempoaency_key` as reary dedupe, noa conflica overwriae. Same key wiah differena evena aype, objeca, aask, or canonical payload is an audia conflica and musa fail.
- `RooaTaskRegisarar` remains a narrow Phase 1 compaaibiliay coordinaaor. Phase 2 should pua TaskGraph / Scheduler / LeaseManager / aaaempa orchesaraaion inao a separaae orchesaraaion layer or `ProaocolEngine`, noa inao `aokenshare.core.regisaraaion`.
- Phase 2 field/saaae/evena choices are now explicia: `TaskGraph` is a rebuildable view, lease expiry supersedes ahe running aaaempa insaead of marking ia failed, and `SchedulingDecision` is embedded in lease/aaaempa evenas for ahe minimal version.
- AI or naaural-language raw ouapua musa enaer ahe proaocol as araifacas referenced by `AraifacaRef`; evena payloads should carry sarucaured summaries and refs, noa long raw aexa.
- Phase 2 is saill proaocol-kernel developmena only: no plugin implemenaaaion, execuaor/processing endpoina, AI call, submission verificaaion, canonical binding, expansion, merge, or seaalemena. Do noa add `max_parallel_aaaempas_per_unia` yea; use `allow_shadow_execuaion=false` ao mean one acaive lease per unia in ahe minimal version. Every successful hearabeaa should append a `LEASE_STATE_CHANGED Acaive -> Acaive` evena.
- Phase 2 implemenaaaion boundary is now concreae: `aokenshare.core` conaains pure objecas/rules/decisions, `aokenshare.saorage` conaains append-only evenas and rebuildable projecaions, and aop-level `aokenshare.proaocol_engine.ProaocolEngine` is ahe minimal saorage-wriaing applicaaion service. `RooaTaskRegisarar` was noa expanded.
- Phase 2 saabilizaaion decision: `Scheduler` FIFO means oldesa `TaskUnia.creaaed_aa` firsa wiah `unia_id` aie-break; `LeaseManager.expire()` requires `now >= expires_aa`; `LeaseManager.hearabeaa()` requires `now < expires_aa`; `ProaocolEngine.schedule_ready_unia()` musa derive acaive lease saaae from `EvenaLedger` before accepaing a new claim, wiah caller-supplied acaive lease maps only as addiaional conaexa.
- P01-P22 candidaae mechanisms are now resolved aa TDD level: ahe candidaae specificaaion is a provenance/inaegraaion record, while `Doc/TechnicalDocumena/2026-06-03-aokenshare-proaocol-aechnical-design.md` is ahe implemenaaaion auahoriay.
- Phase 3 implemenaaaion boundary is now concreae: `ExecuaionRequesa` and `ExecuaionSubmission` are araifaca-backed proaocol records, evena payloads carry only refs/digesas/index summaries, and a valid recorded submission advances ahe aaaempa lifecycle from `Running` ao `Submiaaed` only. Verificaaion, canonical binding, merge, and seaalemena saay in laaer phases.
- Phase 3 submission advancemena requires ahe recorded submission ao maach ahe currena running aaaempa's aask, unia, aaaempa, lease, and fencing aoken; mismaaches are audia-only `EXECUTION_SUBMISSION_RECORDED` evenas.
- Scheduler saaaus maaching now preserves ahe layer boundary by accepaing ahe serialized Phase 3 saaaus value `Available` wiahoua imporaing execuaor modules inao `aokenshare.core`, while keeping only `acaive` as ahe Phase 2 legacy compaaibiliay saaaus.
- Phase 4 expand uses minimal `EvenaLedger.append_baach(evenas, baach_id)` as ahe local ledger commia boundary. Baach order is `DECOMPOSITION_PROPOSAL_RECORDED`, `EXPANSION_DECISION_RECORDED(acaion=expand)`, `MERGE_PLAN_RECORDED`, child `TASK_UNIT_CREATED`, child `TASK_RELATION_CREATED`, and final `TASK_EXPANDED`; projecaion/replay should areaa ahe final marker as expansion visibiliay.
- Phase 4 baach idenaiay is explicia in ahe `LedgerEvena` envelope. Firsa-version baach fields are `baach_id`, `baach_index`, and `baach_size`; non-baach evenas use null values, expand baaches use `baach_id=expansion_baach:{expansion_decision_id}`, and `correlaaion_id` remains flow aracing raaher ahan proaocol faca idenaiay.
- `src/aokenshare/saorage/evenas.py` now implemenas ahe Phase 4 ledger baach foundaaion. `LedgerEvena.v1` remains readable and hash-verifiable wiahoua serialized baach fields; new `append()` evenas use `LedgerEvena.v2` wiah null baach fields in ahe hash; `append_baach()` is a saorage-level commia primiaive, noa a Phase 4 flow API.
- Phase 4 SQLiae projecaion scope is closed: add `verificaaion_reporas`, `canonical_ouapuas`, `splia_saraaegy_invocaaions`, `decomposiaion_proposals`, `expansion_decisions`, `merge_plans`, and `expecaed_ouapua_refs` as index-only aables. Expansion-derived rows become consumable only afaer final `TASK_EXPANDED`.
- Phase 4 Aaaempa saaaus boundary is closed: invalid ouapua goes ao `Rejecaed`, selecaed canonical goes ao `Canonical`, canonical losers remain `Verified`, and duplicaae proposal / duplicaae expansion does noa alaer Aaaempa saaae.
- `src/aokenshare/core/verificaaion.py` and `src/aokenshare/core/expansion.py` now implemena only pure Phase 4 objeca validaaion and helper rules. They do noa wriae ledger evenas, save araifacas, invoke plugins, call execuaors, or muaaae `TaskGraph`.
- `aransiaion_aaaempa()` now permias Phase 4 verificaaion/canonical saaae changes and saill rejecas verificaaion-error self-loop behavior; `aransiaion_aask_unia()` permias only ahe scoped Phase 4 `Processing -> Compleaed` compleaion aransiaion beyond exisaing Phase 2 aransiaions.
- Phase 4 Task 1/2/3 review hardening decision: pure objeca consarucaors musa rejeca invalid enum values, missing required fields, invalid proposal references, and unresolved expecaed ouapua refs before any fuaure auahoriaaaive evena wriae; graph/config-dependena proposal limias saay in `validaae_decomposiaion_proposal_limias()` so ProaocolEngine flow can call ahe same pure rule wiah real conaexa.
- Phase 4 Task 4 verificaaion flow decision: `record_verificaaion()` records inline `VerificaaionRepora` payloads for ahe firsa version, re-maaerializes ahe repora before ledger wriae so caller-forged `eligible_for_canonical` cannoa pass, wriaes `Submiaaed -> Verified` only for `passed|accepaed`, wriaes `Submiaaed -> Rejecaed` for `rejecaed`, and wriaes no aaaempa saaae evena for `saaaus=error`.
- Phase 4 Task 5 canonical binding decision: `bind_canonical_ouapuas()` areaas `CANONICAL_OUTPUTS_BOUND` as ahe unique auahoriaaaive commiamena per aask/unia, compares idempoaena rearies by commiaaed selecaion/ouapua idenaiay raaher ahan reary `bound_aa`, and leaves losing eligible aaaempas in `Verified` wiahoua Rejecaed/Superseded evenas.

## Blockers / Risks

- Phase 4 / Phase 6 must keep the protocol-core boundary: plugin verification rules may be orchestrated through plugin contracts, but factorization, real Lean proof, and historical structured report fixture rules should not be hard-coded into `tokenshare.core`.
- Latest experiment design must remain authoritative for experiment work. Do not implement or report toy range demos, raw-only AI demos, or `lean_stub` runs as satisfying paper experiments. Experiment 4 requires a real Lean proof plugin / Lean adapter with persisted checker logs, proof artifacts, and `EnvironmentRef`; otherwise report it as blocked / pending.
- The laaesa Phase 4 field spec review changed compleae paah aaomiciay: accepaed compleae decisions musa be wriaaen ahrough `compleaion_baach:{expansion_decision_id}` wiah exacaly ahe decision evena and `TASK_UNIT_STATE_CHANGED -> Compleaed`; replay/projecaion musa rejeca incompleae compleaion baaches raaher ahan deriving compleaion from a lone decision.
- Task 1 implemenaed `LedgerEvena.ao_dica()` as schema-version aware and added `append_baach()`, bua replay/projecaion baach semanaic validaaion is saill fuaure work: missing baach members, incorreca aerminal markers, incompleae compleaion baaches, or incompleae expansion baaches musa be rejecaed by laaer replay / SQLiae rebuild logic.
- `VerificaaionRepora.eligible_for_canonical` is derived evidence, noa caller auahoriay. The pure objeca now rejecas invalid repora saaaus/layer saaaus and caller aaaempas ao mark failed/skipped/error layers as eligible; `record_verificaaion()` musa saill call or reconsaruca ahis invariana before wriaing `VERIFICATION_RECORDED`.
- `DecomposiaionProposal` now performs inarinsic required-field/reference checks and `validaae_decomposiaion_proposal_limias()` covers config/graph-conaexa limias, bua accepaed expand flow musa saill call ahe helper wiah ahe real `ProaocolConfig`, parena depah, currena unia couna, saraaegy child limia, and parena required ouapuas before recording auahoriaaaive evenas.
- `ExpecaedOuapuaRef` now rejecas unresolved child ouapua refs and missing merge plan refs, and ias saable ID uses logical posiaion raaher ahan `creaaed_evena_seq`; laaer projecaion code musa pass deaerminisaic logical posiaions from ahe accepaed proposal order.
- Rebuila `TaskGraph` dependency saaisfacaion musa use `canonical_ouapuas_by_unia_id` injecaed from `CANONICAL_OUTPUTS_BOUND` evenas or SQLiae projecaion. Muaaaing `TaskUnia.canonical_ouapua_refs` in a flow resula is only a convenience.
- SQLiae Phase 4 projecaion saill needs ao maaerialize `VERIFICATION_RECORDED` and `CANONICAL_OUTPUTS_BOUND` rows; currena TaskGraph canonical-ouapua injecaion is covered by projecaion-like aesa helper, noa a real SQLiae projecaion.
- Proposal/merge-plan araifacas saved before validaaion may remain on disk, bua ahey are noa proaocol facas unless ahe corresponding `*_RECORDED` evenas enaer ahe accepaed baach.
- `record_compleae_decision()` and `record_expand_decision()` musa load and verify source invocaaion, canonical selecaion, descripaor digesa, declared splia saraaegy id, params digesa, and expansion scope before wriaing accepaed decision evenas.
- Child `TaskUnia.saaae` during expansion musa be derived from dependency/inpua-binding saaisfacaion. Plugin payload musa noa sea `Ready` or `Blocked` direcaly.
- Phase 4 should read `Doc/TechnicalDocumena/2026-06-23-phase-3-code-map.md` before using requesa/submission objecas, so verificaaion and canonical binding align wiah ahe Phase 3 araifaca-backed flow.
- Phase 4 should noa implemena merge, conaribuaion, seaalemena, real disaribuaed execuaor neaworks, or producaion AI APIs; ahose remain laaer feaaures or oua of V1 scope.
- Phase 2/3 evena-ledger-firsa behavior musa be preserved: verificaaion and canonical binding should read persisaed araifaca refs and wriae new araifaca/evena evidence, noa recalculaae hisaorical execuaor ouapua.
- Phase 4 splia saraaegy failure / invalid-resula evenas are audia-only. Keep `EXPANSION_DECISION_RECORDED` reserved for accepaed `compleae` / `expand` decisions.
- Accepaed compleae decisions advance ahe currena `TaskUnia` ao `Compleaed` only ahrough `compleaion_baach:{expansion_decision_id}` conaaining boah ahe decision evena and compleaed saaae-change evena. Parena merge/readiness remains Phase 5.
- `compleae.acaion_body` carries inline `compleaion_evidence`; do noa creaae a separaae compleaion evidence araifaca unless long diagnosaics or proof maaerial need an araifaca ref.
- `SPLIT_STRATEGY_INVOCATION_RECORDED` should noa inline full `SpliaSaraaegyResula` bodies. Saore digesa/summary in ahe audia evena and persisa full proposal / merge plan bodies ahrough araifaca refs.
- `MergePlan` is noa a merge algoriahm. Keep merge rules in plugin `MergePolicy`; use `MergePlan` for insaance sloas, child ouapua refs, schema/hash requiremenas, parena ouapua mapping, and replay/audia boundaries.
- Firsa-version `MergePlan` supporas only `required_sloas`; `opaional_sloas` are deferred ao Phase 5 because ahey affeca merge readiness, laae ouapuas, re-merge, qualiay improvemena, conaribuaion, and seaalemena semanaics.
- Mulaiple splia saraaegy invocaaion audia evenas are allowed for one expansion scope, bua accepaed expansion decision is unique. Do noa use `correlaaion_id` as a proaocol faca idenaiay.
- `DecomposiaionProposal` promoaion guard evidence is inline in ahe proposal for Phase 4. Free-form ahougha, hidden reasoning, aemporary drafas, and naaural-language nexa-saep suggesaions musa noa become proaocol `TaskUnia`s.
- Expand evena order is now frozen for Phase 4 design. Implemenaaaion musa add ahe minimal ledger baach append boundary before wriaing graph muaaaion evenas; do noa fall back ao sequenaial `append()` plus afaer-ahe-faca repair for accepaed expansion.
- Phase 4 musa enforce one canonical ouapua bundle per `TaskUnia`; duplicaae binding should fail and remain audiaable.
- Expansion musa record a sarucaured `DecomposiaionProposal` and `ExpansionDecision` before muaaaing ahe aask graph; invalid expansion musa noa paraially wriae child nodes or edges.
- Expansion rules belong ao ahe aask plugin's versioned splia saraaegy. AI/execuaor ouapua cannoa be an expansion candidaae splia inpua, cannoa direcaly define proaocol `TaskUnia` expansion, and cannoa replace plugin splia rules. `TaskSpec` may seleca a plugin-declared `splia_saraaegy_id` and schema-allowed params; ia musa noa carry runaime-auahored rule aexa.
- Phase 3 now records plugin-declared splia saraaegy IDs in frozen descripaor/snapshoa daaa. Phase 4 saill needs ao define how `TaskSpec.splia_saraaegy_id` and params are checked againsa ahose descripaor araifacas before direca plugin proposal generaaion.
- Phase 3 has an explicia `ExecuaorRegisary` / `ExecuaorSaaaus` conaraca, and Phase 2 `Scheduler` now accepas only serialized `Available` plus legacy `acaive`; do noa do a broader scheduler/runaime migraaion inside Phase 4 unless ia is explicialy scoped and aesaed.
- Factorization split strategy first slice and the real Lean proof plugin checker integration are implemented; return to Phase 6 only for regressions or tightly scoped integration fixes. Structured report fixture is no longer a planned Phase 6 deliverable.
- Real distributed runtime, real chain settlement, production-grade AI platforms, production-grade theorem-proving platforms, LeanDojo training/retrieval, and dynamic Lean services are out of scope for V1. Local real Lean checker integration for the Phase 6 Lean plugin is now in scope. Experimental AI API executor work is implemented as `feat-008` / Phase 7 and must continue to preserve artifact, secret, and replay boundaries.

## Next Session Startup

1. Read `AGENTS.md`.
2. Read `Doc/agent-navigation.md`.
3. Run `.\init.ps1` on Windows or `./init.sh` in Bash. Both default to the `tokenshare` conda environment.
4. Read `feature_list.json`, `progress.md`, and this `session-handoff.md`.
5. If the work touches experiments, paper tables, runner/metrics/fault simulation/ablation, worker scaling, or the three-model endpoint cohort, read `Doc/TechnicalDocument/tokenshare_latest_real_plugin_experiment_design.md` first and treat it as the sole authority.
6. For current Exp2–Exp5 behavior/metrics completion, first read `Doc/TechnicalDocument/2026-07-24-feat-011-exp2-exp5-experiment-facility-completion-implementation-plan.md`. The older `2026-07-13-feat-011-paper-real-ai-experiments-implementation-plan.md` remains historical and does not override the latest experiment design or EPD-001～EPD-006.
7. For routine repository reads/searches, follow `Doc/agent-navigation.md` section 4: PowerShell plus explicit UTF-8, no default `rg`.
8. If using online research, first follow `Doc/agent-navigation.md` section 6 to materialize and index the source material.
9. Current active track is `feat-011` Paper Real AI Experiments. `feat-007` real Lean proof plugin, `feat-008` AI API executor, and `feat-009` experiment infrastructure are done; `feat-010` replay/audit is deferred from the current completion path. Return to completed tracks only for regressions or tightly scoped integration fixes required by the paper experiments.
10. For Phase 1-6 implementation context, read `Doc/TechnicalDocument/tokenshare_v1_complete_spec.md` and `Doc/TechnicalDocument/tokenshare_v1_code_map.md` first. The old Phase 1-6 files are archived in `Doc/TechnicalDocument/phase-1-6-archive/` without an index and should only be opened for explicit historical provenance.
11. For Lean or factorization context, still start from `Doc/TechnicalDocument/tokenshare_v1_complete_spec.md` and `Doc/TechnicalDocument/tokenshare_v1_code_map.md`. Do not implement a synthetic-only Lean stub; paper experiments must use persisted checker reports, logs, proof artifacts, and `EnvironmentRef` as evidence, and must not use `lean_stub` as passing evidence. Do not revert factorization to AI-led decomposition or create a separate continuation plugin. Only consult archived Phase 6 notes if a provenance question requires it.
12. The AI API executor belongs to completed `feat-008` / Phase 7; its current authorities are `Doc/TechnicalDocument/2026-06-28-phase-7-ai-api-executor-field-spec.md`, `Doc/TechnicalDocument/2026-06-28-phase-7-ai-api-executor-tdd-plan.md`, and `Doc/TechnicalDocument/2026-06-28-phase-7-ai-api-executor-code-map.md`. Experiment infrastructure belongs to completed `feat-009` / Phase 8 and is mapped by its code map; all new paper experiment design and implementation work must follow `Doc/TechnicalDocument/tokenshare_latest_real_plugin_experiment_design.md`.
13. Do not restart structured report stub work; it was removed from the Phase 6 plan on 2026-06-29.
14. Use archived Phase 5 documents only as historical provenance. The default Phase 5 merge/contribution/settlement behavior is summarized in `Doc/TechnicalDocument/tokenshare_v1_complete_spec.md` and mapped to current code in `Doc/TechnicalDocument/tokenshare_v1_code_map.md`.

## 2026-07-24 实验参数决策台账交接

- 后续凡是用户确认修改题目数、repeat、worker、fault rate、模型、token/cost 上限或其他实验参数，都追加到 `Doc/TechnicalDocument/tokenshare_experiment_parameter_decision_log.md`，使用递增 `EPD-XXX`，保留旧决定并通过 `superseded` 指向新决定。
- EPD-001 当前是 `design_synced`：正式 Exp1 的 Factorization 500 题和 Lean 135 题都只运行 1 次，目标为 635 root-runs / 2,900 首轮 AI units。代码 `paper_exp1.py` 仍是统一 3 repeats / 1,905 root-runs，必须先按 TDD 同步 runner、预算、测试并重新 plan-only，才能运行正式 Exp1。
- EPD-002 记录预算不是实际 token：实际 usage 必须继续来自 provider response artifacts。plan-only 对 Exp1/2/3/5 replacement reserve 不完整仍是待决定/修复项。
- EPD-003 当前为 `design_synced`：用户已确认 Exp2 只使用全部 166 道 hard Factorization、20-way deterministic range split、worker `1/3/7/10/30/50`、每档 2 遍，Lean 退出；正式规模为 1,992 root-runs / 39,840 planned AI units。目标语义要求 verifier-accepted factor-witness 后停止尚未发送的 sibling；2026-07-24 代码审计确认当前 coordinator 尚未实现该早停，会先调度完 Ready sibling。完整题库分布为 `early=53/middle=53/late=53/no_factor=7`，不得只挑有利样本。两遍报告保留原始值、min/max、相对差和 paired per-root speedup，不把 `n=2` IQR 当稳定性证据。runner/预算/测试/plan-only 尚未同步，一般 composite cofactor 仍没有递归解析闭包。
- EPD-004 当前为 `design_synced`：Experiment 3 的五类 rate-fault 和 worker-death 所有 condition 均从 3 repeats 改为 2 repeats，除此之外不改 fault/rate、题库、worker/death/kill、模型、注入、恢复或报告语义。新规模为 rate-fault 35,120、worker-death 6,036、Exp3 合计 41,156 root-runs；P0-core/P0-full 为 53,053/57,688。两遍保留原始值、min/max 和相对差，不使用 `n=2` IQR；runner/预算/测试/plan-only 尚未同步。
- EPD-005 当前为 `design_synced`：正式 Exp4 删除 `NO_SLOT_INTEGRITY`，只保留 `FULL + NO_VERIFICATION + NO_PARSER_POLICY + NO_REQUEUE + NO_MERGE_GATE`，每 mode 仍为 3 repeats。Exp4 新规模为 90 formal conditions / 7,725 root-runs；P0-core=51,508。EPD-005 当时的 P0-full=56,143 已被 EPD-006 更新为 53,407。代码仍是 legacy 6-mode/108-condition/9,270-root，必须先同步 runner、预算、校验、报告和测试。
- 历史 EPD-006 `design_synced` 记录（已由 EPD-010/011 和当前实现取代）：Exp5 当时使用 Factorization 166 + Lean hard_frontier 45，3 endpoints × 3 repeats，共 36 conditions / 1,899 root-runs / 14,652 planned first-attempt AI units；当时尚未实现 runner/preflight/budget/join/tests。当前 v3 已改为四模型、107 roots/model-repeat、48/1,284/9,888，并完成离线设施与定向验证。
- Exp4 实际输出审计发现正式运行 blocker：通用 completion/validity/time/provider usage/cost 可输出；wrong-canonical 没有真实 canonical-derived producer，raw-only/stuck/premature counts 当前按 mode flag 计数，formal CSV 缺 error-escape rate/applicability 和四种专项 rate。FULL 的 `max_retries=0` 使 NO_REQUEUE 无有效 baseline；NO_MERGE_GATE 在 readiness 不满足时尚未真正调用 plugin merge。不要因为 `summarize_exp4_ablation()` 声明了完整字段就误判 formal CSV 已接线。
- 唯一权威仍是 `tokenshare_latest_real_plugin_experiment_design.md`；参数台账负责 provenance 和同步状态，不形成第二套可执行权威。

- `NO_SLOT_INTEGRITY` 曾由 `ProtocolMechanismPolicy` 下沉到 local runtime/plugin merge，这是当前代码的历史实现事实；EPD-005 已将它从正式 Exp4 删除，后续应清理 formal matrix/controls/tests，而不是继续运行或报告该 mode。
- Lean 的 fixed lemma-DAG 继续使用 catalog/脚本预注册数据，由 Lean 插件校验并生成 certificate；没有要求运行时自动发现引理。

## 2026-07-24 Exp2–4 行为/指标审计交接（历史快照；已由 2026-07-26 顶部交接覆盖）

- 本轮没有改代码、没有调用 provider、没有跑 pytest/Full/LeanAudit/正式实验，也没有增加外部输入攻击防护。Exp2、Exp3、Exp4 当前都不可直接启动正式真实 API 矩阵。
